  • Cincinnati Bengals @ Atlanta Falcons

    Today's Analysis · 6 picks

    6:30 AM

    Where to watch: NFL Network (International game) · TV listings ↗

    Today's Analysis

    Updated Nov 7, 8:02 PM PT

    Madrid game at the Bernabéu: Cincinnati are about 3-point favourites with a 52.5–53 total. Joe Burrow (#9) has 20 TD passes in 7 games against an average Atlanta pass defence, while Bijan Robinson (#7) has gone 48 carries without a score over three weeks. The one game-line edge is Falcons +3.5 at NoVig. The props lean on usage: Cincinnati throwing (Burrow TDs, Tee Higgins (#5) yards) and Atlanta's secondary options held down.

    • Injuries (final reports Friday): Cincinnati S Kyle Dugger out (quad), DE Cedric Johnson questionable (ankle, full practice), CB DJ Turner II cleared; WR Andrei Iosivas (#80) on IR. Atlanta RB Brian Robinson Jr. (#15, hamstring) and WR Zachariah Branch (#17, hamstring) out; LB Divine Deablo, DE James Pearce Jr. and T Cameron Williams questionable. Official inactives come about 90 minutes before the 6:30 AM PT kickoff.
    • Brian Robinson Jr. out leaves Bijan Robinson (#7) with Khalil Herbert (#25) behind him, the same setup as Weeks 7–8 (Bijan 20 and 19 carries for 63 and 76 yards; Herbert 9 and 12 carries).
    • Venue: Santiago Bernabéu, Madrid, with a retractable roof; no roof decision or forecast found (early-November averages are 8–16°C). Neutral-site game with Atlanta the designated home team, so home-field is close to nothing.
    • Market: Pinnacle CIN −3 (no-vig 51.9% to cover), ML no-vig CIN 61.5% / ATL 38.5%, total 53 (Over 49.4% no-vig). The total opened 49.5 and rose three points. Implied team totals about CIN 28, ATL 25. No ⚑ on any line or prop.
    • Efficiency (EPA/play, season): CIN offense +0.115 (pass +0.223, run −0.088, 65% pass rate) vs ATL defence −0.035 (pass −0.002, run −0.082). ATL offense −0.095 (pass −0.161, run −0.024) vs CIN defence −0.008. Red-zone TD rate: CIN 62% on offence and 40% allowed; ATL 64% and 63% allowed. Context only, not an edge.
    • First-TD market: team scores first CIN 55.8%, ATL 43.5%. Dropped as thin or stale: both team-defence quotes at FanDuel/Bovada, Ke'Shawn Williams at NoVig and No Scorer at Fanatics.
    • App model (season EPA, LOW confidence): ATL 47.7% to win and 55.6% to cover +3, against the market's 38.5% and ~48%. The models trail the close, so this is not used as an edge.
    • Previews split: Yahoo CIN 31-24, SI CIN 30-20, CBS lead pick ATL 34-30, SBR CIN 28-26 with Over 52.5.
    1. 1

      Falcons +3.5 (−111) -111 Novig

      Line shopping: Pinnacle's −3 (no-vig 51.9% Bengals cover, roughly 9% push on 3) implies about 52.8% for Atlanta +3.5, a fair price near −112, and NoVig pays −111 while BetMGM and Fanatics charge −118 and −120. The half point over the key number is the edge; small but real. Only three books hang 3.5.

    2. 2

      Joe Burrow (#9 · avg 2.9 pass TD/g) Over 2.5 pass TDs (+145) +145 BetMGM

      Usage: 3+ TD passes in 4 of 7 games, including 4, 4 and 5 in the last three, against a 40% fair price. Atlanta allow TDs on 63% of red-zone trips, and Cincinnati run poorly (−0.088 EPA per carry) and finish drives through the air. Not a measured edge.

    3. 3

      Tee Higgins (#5 · avg 103.7 rec yds/g) Over 77.5 rec yds (−106) -106 Novig

      Usage: over 77.5 in 5 of 7 games (103.7 a game, 8.7 targets) and 148 and 155 in his last two. Iosivas (#80) on IR keeps the targets concentrated on Ja'Marr Chase (#1) and Higgins. Not a measured edge.

    4. 4

      Bijan Robinson (#7 · avg 97.5 rush yds/g) Under 97.5 rush yds (−102) -102 Novig

      Usage: the 97.5 average leans on 194 and 145 in Weeks 3–4; he went under in 6 of 8 (median 79.5) and in both games without Brian Robinson Jr. (63, 76) despite ~20 carries, because Herbert (#25) takes 9–12. Atlanta trailing would also cut the run game. Not a measured edge.

    5. 5

      Kyle Pitts (#8 · avg 24.6 rec yds/g) Under 42.5 rec yds (+102) +102 Novig

      Usage: over 42.5 in only 2 of 8 games (24.6 a game, 4 targets), topping out at 60. The line prices a role he hasn't had, and Atlanta's pass offence is −0.161 EPA per dropback. Not a measured edge.

    6. 6

      Ja'Marr Chase (#1 · 6 TD in 7 g) anytime TD (−106) -106 Novig

      Usage: 6 TDs in 7 games and 5 inside-10 chances, on a ~28-point team total; NoVig −106 is right on the 51% fair price, so this is a likelihood play, not value.

    Top anytime TD scorers

    Parlay: Burrow (#9) Over 2.5 TDs, Higgins (#5) Over 77.5 and Chase (#1) anytime TD all ride Cincinnati's passing game and correlate positively; Bijan (#7) Under and Pitts (#8) Under fit the same script (Atlanta's offence held down). Falcons +3.5 points the other way: it is a price bet on the half point, not part of that script, so keep it a single. A same-game parlay must be built at one book, which prices the correlation and adds vig, and most of these best prices sit at NoVig and BetMGM, so singles keep the edges.

    Falcons +3.5 is the only measured edge type here (line shopping), and a thin one; the props are usage picks, which are not measured edges. Size everything small. Inactives are not out yet (due about 5 AM PT). Pass on Bijan (#7) Under if Herbert (#25) is inactive, and on the Cincinnati receiver legs if Higgins (#5) or Chase (#1) is a surprise inactive.
